From 9279c642836e979212818786973a8bf1ff29e0f3 Mon Sep 17 00:00:00 2001
From: PoorInfz <cryptocharliesp@gmail.com>
Date: Thu, 10 Sep 2026 21:58:39 +0200
Subject: [PATCH] fix(merge): resolve the botched release/v0.42.0 merge into
 this branch

HEAD carried an unresolved merge (b80ebf4f70, merging df2ae9880f / PR
#3944 / release/v0.42.0 into feature/buddy-companion-system) with raw
`<<<<<<< HEAD` conflict markers committed across 47+ files: the repo did
not compile, and every test file that touched a conflicted area failed
with TS1185 (merge conflict marker encountered). This resolves it end to
end -- source, tests, JSON evidence fixtures, and generated artifacts --
and verifies the result rather than trusting the text merge.

Resolution approach, file by file:
- Declarative content (ItemKind/ItemDef unions, dev command lists,
  keybind/side-button tables, i18n locale overlays, the item-art
  provenance ledger): union both sides' additions.
- Restructured shared logic where one side's rewrite fully subsumed the
  other (nameplate dot-scale painter deps, the mount presentation
  extraction in renderer.ts, the dungeonRarePlusLootIds/admits refactor):
  took the newer structure and re-threaded the older side's addition
  through it (the buddy exclusion, the showPetNames wiring, Forgemaw's
  independent draw appended to the re-cut Ignivar/Varkhul loot groups).
- Frozen historical evidence (docs/achievements/item-art-consistency
  verdict + accepted-art.json, 140,013 bytes / sha cc7a2254...): took
  upstream's committed blob verbatim rather than hand-merging binary-
  adjacent JSON, since the tooling that produced it has since moved to a
  newer evidence directory and can no longer regenerate at that path.
- Live/generated artifacts (i18n resolved tables, parity goldens): re-ran
  the actual generators (`npm run i18n:gen`, `UPDATE_PARITY=1 npx vitest
  run tests/parity`) against the reconciled tree rather than merging their
  output by hand.
- Pinned exact-count assertions (IWORLD_MEMBERS, ALL_DELTA_KEYS,
  COMMAND_NAMES, monolith line-count ceilings, MOBS/ITEMS catalog sizes):
  measured directly off the reconciled tree per each test's own "measured,
  never reconciled by arithmetic" doctrine, not derived from either
  parent's pin.

Also caught two classes of merge fallout that carried no conflict marker
of their own (git auto-merged the surrounding hunks) but were still
broken or stale on the reconciled tree:
- Dangling imports whose call site moved in an unconflicted hunk
  (isControllableOwnedPet, bopPartyTradeInstance, applyTalentMods, five
  weapon/mount-skin imports in server/game.ts) and two wholesale-replaced
  blocks (hud.ts's refreshKeybindLabels, renderer.ts's mount presentation)
  whose extracted destination module was missing the buddy branch's own
  addition (#mm-collections, the `collections` keybind label).
- Filters written before the buddy system existed that excluded `kind:
  'mount'` from a loot/relic sweep without also excluding the newer `kind:
  'buddy'` (a dungeon boss's gear-drop cap test, two Reliquary
  catalogueable-id derivations), and catalog-size pins (MOBS untagged
  count, HEROIC_VENDOR_STOCK-derived shop view) that hardened before the
  32 buddy mob templates / whistle items existed.

One real content gap surfaced and fixed along the way: whistle_emberfall_
phoenix's icon was never rendered (render_buddy_item_icons.mjs needs a
headless-Chromium host this sandbox does not have -- confirmed by trying
it). Declared honestly via a new BUDDY_ART_PENDING_ITEM_IDS ledger
(src/sim/content/buddies.ts) rather than left to 404, with the render
script's own item list updated so the one command reaches it whenever a
host that can run it does.

Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean repo-wide; `git grep '^<<<<<<<'` empty;
`npm run i18n:gen` regenerated cleanly; the full parity suite (262 tests)
passes on the regenerated goldens; every test file this touched passes
(1633 tests across 29 files in the final consolidated run), including the
ones with no conflict marker at all that had merely gone stale. Two
pre-existing Windows-sandbox flakes remain in
tests/item_art_audit_builder.test.ts's temp-directory cleanup (EPERM on
rmSync racing a lock after heavy image I/O), confirmed unrelated: an
unrelated test with the same rmSync pattern passes cleanly, and the
file's third (non-cleanup) test passes on its own.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>

+  /** onCharacterAssetReady fires for EVERY character GLB reaching residency
+   *  (every buddy/creature in the post-entry stream, every lazy mount someone
+   *  else just mounted, every weapon skin); a bound instance method (not an
+   *  inline closure) so destroy() can unsubscribe the exact same reference.
+   *  No-ops unless the LAST requested visual is the one that just failed to
+   *  build (currentVisual still null) and this is genuinely the url it was
+   *  waiting on, so an unrelated arrival elsewhere in the world costs one
+   *  cheap identity check here, not a rebuild attempt. */
+  private onCharacterAssetArrived = (url: string): void => {
+    if (this.destroyed || this.currentVisual || !this.pendingVisualRetry) return;
+    if (VISUALS[this.pendingVisualRetry.visualKey]?.url !== url) return;
+    const retry = this.pendingVisualRetry;

+      retry.visualKey,
+      retry.weaponItemId,
+      retry.weaponOverride,
+      retry.offhandItemId,
+      retry.tint,
+    );
+  };
